# Commands · B · part 6

1,447 entries, brainstorm to brief-from-order. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **brainstorm**: Loose, exploratory dialogue for a not-yet-scoped idea -- purpose, rough shape, and alternatives, with no file writes. Epic-scale ideas can convene a pitch-agent panel (minimalist/skeptic/scout) for unanchored perspectives. Hands off to /turnstile:refine once the idea is concrete enough to formalize into a ticket. The pre-refine stage of the turnstile workflow. ([febradc-github/turnstile](https://github.com/febradc-github/turnstile/blob/HEAD/commands/brainstorm.md) · ★1)
- **brainstorm**: Pre-execution preparation pipeline (Kiro-clone integrated). Runs intake → dynamic exhaustive clarification (v6.2+) → alternative-approach brainstorming (v6.2+) → spec lifecycle (init/requirements/validate-gap/design/validate-design/tasks) before optional handoff to /pipeline-orchestrator:pipeline. Mandatory for MEDIA/COMPLEXA/Spec tasks via auto-dispatch from pipeline-controller STEP 1.7. - **break.ko**: AutoCAD 끊기(BREAK, BR), 캐드 브레이크를 프롬프트 순서대로 설명합니다: 선택한 점이 첫 번째 점, 두 번째 점, 첫 번째 점 옵션, 점에서 끊기. 브라우저에서 무료로 해 볼 수 있습니다. ([ovelilla/mellowcad](https://github.com/ovelilla/mellowcad/blob/HEAD/content/commands/break.ko.md))
- **break.pl**: Jak przerwać linię w AutoCADzie poleceniem PRZERWIJ (BREAK, skrót PRZ): wskazanie to pierwszy punkt, potem drugi, albo opcja Pierwszy; przerwanie w punkcie. ([ovelilla/mellowcad](https://github.com/ovelilla/mellowcad/blob/HEAD/content/commands/break.pl.md))
- **break.pt-BR**: Como funciona o BREAK do AutoCAD em português, QUEBRAR (alias QU), prompt a prompt: o clique é o primeiro ponto, depois o segundo, ou Primeiro ponto. ([ovelilla/mellowcad](https://github.com/ovelilla/mellowcad/blob/HEAD/content/commands/break.pt-BR.md))
- **break.ru**: Как разорвать линию в AutoCAD командой РАЗОРВАТЬ (BREAK, РА): точка выбора — первая точка, затем вторая, или опция «Первая»; разрыв в точке. Попробуйте онлайн. ([ovelilla/mellowcad](https://github.com/ovelilla/mellowcad/blob/HEAD/content/commands/break.ru.md))
- **break.zh-CN**: CAD 打断怎么用？BREAK（快捷键 BR）按提示顺序讲解：拾取点即第一个打断点，再指定第二点，或选择第一点；以及在点处打断。在浏览器中免费试用。 ([ovelilla/mellowcad](https://github.com/ovelilla/mellowcad/blob/HEAD/content/commands/break.zh-CN.md))

([kingler/agentic-harness-engineering](https://github.com/kingler/agentic-harness-engineering/blob/HEAD/exercises/app-build-template/.roo/commands/breakout-2-skills-subagents.md))
- **breakout-3-tools-mcp**: BREAKOUT 3 of 5. Plan and build the verb-named tool the skill calls, plus any MCP server it needs. Walks the planning questions, writes the tool script + its WHAT/WHEN/WHEN-NOT description in AGENTS.md, and adds MCP servers to mcp.json. Run after /breakout-2-skills-subagents. Chains to /breakout-4-hooks-rules. ([kingler/agentic-harness-engineering](https://github.com/kingler/agentic-harness-engineering/blob/HEAD/exercises/app-build-template/.roo/commands/breakout-3-tools-mcp.md))
- **breakout-4-hooks-rules**: BREAKOUT 4 of 5. Plan and write the deterministic gate — the pre-tool hook that blocks the nightmare-failure action, plus the hard rule it enforces. Walks the planning questions, then writes pre-tool-use.sh and the rules. Run after /breakout-3-tools-mcp. Chains to /breakout-5-knowledge-memory. ([kingler/agentic-harness-engineering](https://github.com/kingler/agentic-harness-engineering/blob/HEAD/exercises/app-build-template/.roo/commands/breakout-4-hooks-rules.md))
- **breakout-5-knowledge-memory**: BREAKOUT 5 of 5. Plan and write the agent's grounding — durable domain knowledge plus the per-session memory plan. Walks the planning questions, then fills knowledge/domain-notes.md and memory/SESSION.md. Run after /breakout-4-hooks-rules. Chains to Day 2 (/plan-app). ([kingler/agentic-harness-engineering](https://github.com/kingler/agentic-harness-engineering/blob/HEAD/exercises/app-build-template/.roo/commands/breakout-5-knowledge-memory.md))
